# Dependency audit - 2026-09-29

Checked official registries, upstream release tags, runtime support schedules and published dependency contracts on 2026-09-29. Before versions come from current main `aa3a32e54497ce4330d88233dccf733804c1fad3`; ranges and lock resolutions are distinct. Native resolvers preserve upstream constraints and packaged bundle ownership.

## Source PR map

No open PRs, including drafts or human-authored work, existed at discovery. [#379](https://github.com/justinthelaw/justinthelaw/pull/379) had already merged into main; [#376](https://github.com/justinthelaw/justinthelaw/pull/376), [#377](https://github.com/justinthelaw/justinthelaw/pull/377) and [#378](https://github.com/justinthelaw/justinthelaw/pull/378) were already closed and incorporated by #379. This work starts on a fresh September 29 branch from updated main.

## Migration and retained constraints

- TypeScript 7.0.2 is outside the latest parser peer range `>=4.8.4 <6.1.0`; retain 6.0.3 until upstream supports 7. [Published parser metadata](https://registry.npmjs.org/@typescript-eslint/typescript-estree/8.71.0).
- Node 24.21.0 remains latest active LTS; reconsider Node 26 with its types at the October 28 LTS transition. [Official schedule](https://github.com/nodejs/Release#release-schedule).
- Hub 2.0.0 is excluded by current Transformers 5.17, tokenizers 0.23.2 and training datasets 5.0.1; retain latest compatible Hub 1.33.0 until those upstreams permit 2. [Transformers metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/transformers/5.17.0/json).
- filelock 4 changes only the soft read/write lock protocol; this integration uses classic FileLock/SoftFileLock. A real classic lock smoke and full ML suites pass. [Migration](https://github.com/tox-dev/filelock/pull/738).
- urllib3 2.8.0 fixes two high and one medium vulnerabilities in 2.7.0: [proxy TLS](https://github.com/urllib3/urllib3/security/advisories/GHSA-8988-9cw3-xx77), [chunk size memory](https://github.com/urllib3/urllib3/security/advisories/GHSA-vxq7-64xx-v4gw), [Deflate loop](https://github.com/urllib3/urllib3/security/advisories/GHSA-gh4c-6fx4-qh6g).
- Deprecated fix-smartquotes moved to released texthooks 0.7.1; the same hook ID and stages preserve behavior. Byte/exit-code comparison covered Markdown, ASCII, CRLF without EOF, Unicode quote sets and custom arguments.
- Nano ID 6 and protobufjs 8 remain outside the owning PostCSS `^3.3.18` and ONNX runtime `^7.2.4` contracts. Compatible Nano ID 3.3.19 is selected; revisit newer majors when the owners support them.
- Tailwind owns six bundled WASM helper leaves; independent leaf overrides would disregard its published artifact.
- npm 12.1 lock-only update trips [upstream issue #9800](https://github.com/npm/cli/issues/9800) for Tailwind optional bundled registry tarballs. An actual native update and clean install succeeded with security defaults enabled; optional native ONNX binary download was skipped only locally because api.nuget.org DNS is unavailable.

## Local validation

- Node 24.21.0/npm 12.1.0 native install passed with ONNXRUNTIME_NODE_INSTALL=skip for sandbox optional binary download; normal baseline npm ci failed EAI_AGAIN api.nuget.org
- Lint and TypeScript passed; webpack static export passed
- Full flight-check reached browser install; proxy returned zero-byte ZIP archives so local E2E unavailable
- 87-package training and 61-package exporter hash sync and dependency graph checks passed under Python 3.14.7
- Training imports/trainer processing_class/classic FileLock smoke passed; complete 114 tests passed
- Exporter imports and 4 offline native exporter tests passed
- Both exact-lock pip-audit 2.10.1 runs found no vulnerabilities (local --disable-pip --no-deps audits after complete hashed sync)
- npm audit found 0 vulnerabilities
- Both contribution hook stages passed
